## How to read this guide

Every case in the demo set exists to exercise one specific behaviour of the
pipeline. This guide walks each of them from the application arriving to the
decision being recorded.

**Nothing here was typed by hand.** Every figure, rule id, verdict and message
is read out of the knowledge base files or out of the database after a full
run, so the guide cannot drift away from what the system actually does.

Each case has the same ten sub-sections:

| | |
|---|---|
| **A. Classification** | which rule decided what kind of applicant this is, and why the earlier rules did not match |
| **B. Checklist** | every document the case was asked for, and whether it arrived |
| **C. Documents** | the quality verdict on each file, and every value read from it |
| **D. People and ownership** | who is involved, and the ownership arithmetic |
| **E. Checks** | the registry, identity and screening results |
| **F. Risk** | every factor considered, the points, the band, and any floor |
| **G. Decision** | what was offered, what was recorded, and by whom |
| **H. Communications** | every message the applicant received, in full |
| **I. Timeline** | the complete audit trail |
| **J. Dashboard line** | the row an operations team sees |

Some terms appear throughout:

- A **hold** is a lock placed by one step. Only that step, or the named person
  it belongs to, can release it, and the case moves only when every hold is
  clear. Clearing one hold can surface another.
- A **band** is the risk bucket: low, medium, high, critical, or
  *insufficient evidence* when there is not enough to score at all.
- A **hard floor** is a rule that forces a minimum band regardless of the score.
- A **restricted finding** means the case carries something the applicant may
  not be told, so only generic customer wording is available to it.
- The **confidence floor** is the level below which a value read from a
  document goes to a person rather than into a decision.

The eight steps every case passes through, in order: intake, requirement pack,
document quality, extraction, verification, screening, risk assessment,
decision. **Steps 5 and 6 are the paid ones** - a case that stops before them
has cost nothing.

The knowledge base itself is printed in the appendix at the end.


## WAL-ONB-0001 - Kaari Mets

*Freelance graphic design and brand identity services* &middot; sole_trader in EE, registered as `EE-FIE-4410932` &middot; expected usage: One card; up to 4000 EUR per month on software and travel

### A. Classification

Intake reads `kb/applicant_type_rules.csv` in priority order and takes the **first** rule whose conditions all hold. Later rules are never looked at.

The values this application was judged on:

| Value | This application |
|---|---|
| `entity_type` | sole_trader |
| `programme_type` | direct |
| `ownership_layers` | 0 |
| `has_corporate_shareholder` | False |
| `has_trust_or_nominee` | False |

| Rule | Order | Condition in plain words | Would give | What happened |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AT-01 | 1 | programme type is exactly `white_label` | white_label_partner | did not match: programme type was `direct` |
| AT-02 | 2 | entity type is one of `sole_trader`, `freelancer`, `self_employed` | freelancer_sole_trader | **MATCHED - this rule decided the case** |
| AT-03 | 3 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** ownership layers is greater than 1 | complex_corporate_ubo | not checked - an earlier rule already matched |
| AT-04 | 4 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has corporate shareholder is true | complex_corporate_ubo | not checked - an earlier rule already matched |
| AT-05 | 5 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has trust or nominee is true | complex_corporate_ubo | not checked - an earlier rule already matched |
| AT-06 | 6 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` | sme_corporate | not checked - an earlier rule already matched |

**Result: `freelancer_sole_trader`, route `primary`, by rule AT-02.** Individuals trading on their own account (Section 5.1)

**Jurisdiction and entity scope.** `kb/jurisdiction_routing.csv` maps the applicant's country to a rule set and to the Wallester entity that would hold the relationship.

| Country | Rule set | Entity scope | Supported | Rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| EE | EE | wallester_as | true | &mdash; |

### B. Checklist

Step 2 builds this from `kb/requirement_rule.csv`, taking every rule that applies to this applicant type, jurisdiction and entity type. A rule marked *required* must be satisfied before the paid checks in Step 5 will run. A *conditional* rule applies only when the application answers its condition yes; answered no, it is **waived**. An *optional* rule never blocks anything.

### D. People and ownership

A *beneficial owner* is the person who ultimately owns or controls the applicant. Where the holding runs through another company, the percentage is multiplied along the chain rather than read off any one document.

| Id | Name | Role | Date of birth | Nationality | Resident in |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IND-0001 | Kaari Mets | sole trader | 1991-03-14 | EE | EE |

The threshold is **25%**: at or above it, an owner must be verified.

_No beneficial owners are declared on this case._

### G. Decision

`kb/analyst_decision_taxonomy.csv` says which decisions exist at which band and who may record them. A decision outside the band is never offered; a decision the person's role does not allow is refused when they try to record it.

At band **`low`**:

| Decision | Severity rank | At this band | Allowed at | Role needed | Resulting status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| approve | 1 | **offered** | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| conditional_approve | 2 | **offered** | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| withdrawn | 3 | **offered** | low, medium, high, critical, insufficient_evidence | analyst | closed_withdrawn |
| request_more_information | 4 | **offered** | low, medium, high, insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |
| insufficient_evidence | 5 | not offered | insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |
| enhanced_due_diligence | 6 | not offered | medium, high, critical | analyst | enhanced_due_diligence |
| escalate | 7 | not offered | high, critical | compliance | analyst_review_required |
| reject | 8 | not offered | medium, high, critical | analyst | rejected |

**No decision has been recorded on this case.**

### D. People and ownership

A *beneficial owner* is the person who ultimately owns or controls the applicant. Where the holding runs through another company, the percentage is multiplied along the chain rather than read off any one document.

| Id | Name | Role | Date of birth | Nationality | Resident in |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IND-0004 | Marcus Ellersby | director | 1974-01-27 | GB | GB |
| IND-0005 | Roisin Delamere | director | 1982-05-09 | IE | GB |
| IND-0006 | Torvald Kellingray | ubo | 1969-08-22 | GB | GB |

The threshold is **25%**: at or above it, an owner must be verified.

| Id | Owner | Control | Path | The multiplication | Declared | Verified? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UBO-0001 | Torvald Kellingray | direct shareholding | Calderwick Logistics Ltd | 60% held directly | 60% | verified |

### G. Decision

`kb/analyst_decision_taxonomy.csv` says which decisions exist at which band and who may record them. A decision outside the band is never offered; a decision the person's role does not allow is refused when they try to record it.

At band **`medium`**:

| Decision | Severity rank | At this band | Allowed at | Role needed | Resulting status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| approve | 1 | **offered** | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| conditional_approve | 2 | **offered** | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| withdrawn | 3 | **offered** | low, medium, high, critical, insufficient_evidence | analyst | closed_withdrawn |
| request_more_information | 4 | **offered** | low, medium, high, insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |
| insufficient_evidence | 5 | not offered | insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |
| enhanced_due_diligence | 6 | **offered** | medium, high, critical | analyst | enhanced_due_diligence |
| escalate | 7 | not offered | high, critical | compliance | analyst_review_required |
| reject | 8 | **offered** | medium, high, critical | analyst | rejected |

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Decision | **request_more_information** |
| Recorded by | R. Toome (analyst) |
| Reason code | registered_address_mismatch |
| Rationale | Registry and applicant documents disagree on the registered address. Everything else corroborates. Asking the applicant to confirm the current registered address and provide a document that shows it, rather than escalating, because no screening or identity issue is present. |
| Evidence relied on | RSK-0002, REG-0002, EVP-0002 |
| Escalation target | &mdash; |
| Override? | yes |
| Override direction | stricter |
| Override reason | Registry and applicant documents disagree on the registered address. Everything else corroborates. Asking the applicant to confirm the current registered address and provide a document that shows it, rather than escalating, because no screening or identity issue is present. |

An **override** means the decision differed from the recommendation. The system recommended `conditional_approve` (severity rank 2) and the reviewer chose `request_more_information` (rank 4), so the direction is **stricter**. The reviewer does not declare this; the system computes it from the severity order.

## WAL-ONB-0004 - Vestmark Nordic OU

*Import and distribution of marine equipment* &middot; private_limited_company in EE, registered as `EE-90012345` &middot; expected usage: Up to 15 cards; around 35000 EUR per month

### A. Classification

Intake reads `kb/applicant_type_rules.csv` in priority order and takes the **first** rule whose conditions all hold. Later rules are never looked at.

The values this application was judged on:

| Value | This application |
|---|---|
| `entity_type` | private_limited_company |
| `programme_type` | direct |
| `ownership_layers` | 3 |
| `has_corporate_shareholder` | True |
| `has_trust_or_nominee` | False |

| Rule | Order | Condition in plain words | Would give | What happened |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AT-01 | 1 | programme type is exactly `white_label` | white_label_partner | did not match: programme type was `direct` |
| AT-02 | 2 | entity type is one of `sole_trader`, `freelancer`, `self_employed` | freelancer_sole_trader | did not match: entity type was `private_limited_company` |
| AT-03 | 3 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** ownership layers is greater than 1 | complex_corporate_ubo | **MATCHED - this rule decided the case** |
| AT-04 | 4 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has corporate shareholder is true | complex_corporate_ubo | not checked - an earlier rule already matched |
| AT-05 | 5 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has trust or nominee is true | complex_corporate_ubo | not checked - an earlier rule already matched |
| AT-06 | 6 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` | sme_corporate | not checked - an earlier rule already matched |

**Result: `complex_corporate_ubo`, route `primary`, by rule AT-03.** Indirect ownership chain needs enhanced UBO handling (Sections 5.1 and 14.4)

**Jurisdiction and entity scope.** `kb/jurisdiction_routing.csv` maps the applicant's country to a rule set and to the Wallester entity that would hold the relationship.

| Country | Rule set | Entity scope | Supported | Rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| EE | EE | wallester_as | true | &mdash; |

### D. People and ownership

A *beneficial owner* is the person who ultimately owns or controls the applicant. Where the holding runs through another company, the percentage is multiplied along the chain rather than read off any one document.

| Id | Name | Role | Date of birth | Nationality | Resident in |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IND-0007 | Lembit Vaher | director | 1971-02-11 | EE | EE |
| IND-0008 | Anneli Sormus | director | 1980-07-30 | EE | EE |
| IND-0009 | Ruben Halvorsen | ubo | 1966-12-05 | NO | NO |

The threshold is **25%**: at or above it, an owner must be verified.

| Id | Owner | Control | Path | The multiplication | Declared | Verified? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UBO-0002 | Anneli Sormus | direct shareholding | Vestmark Nordic OU | 24.5% held directly | 24.5% | verified |
| UBO-0003 | Ruben Halvorsen | indirect shareholding | Harboe Holdings OU > Lindval Mid Holdings SA > Vestmark Nordic OU | 70% x 45% = **31.5%** | 31.5% | unverified **- at or above the threshold and not verified** |

```
8 + 15 + 25 + 6 + 15 = 69
```

### G. Decision

`kb/analyst_decision_taxonomy.csv` says which decisions exist at which band and who may record them. A decision outside the band is never offered; a decision the person's role does not allow is refused when they try to record it.

At band **`high`**:

| Decision | Severity rank | At this band | Allowed at | Role needed | Resulting status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| approve | 1 | not offered | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| conditional_approve | 2 | not offered | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| withdrawn | 3 | **offered** | low, medium, high, critical, insufficient_evidence | analyst | closed_withdrawn |
| request_more_information | 4 | **offered** | low, medium, high, insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |
| insufficient_evidence | 5 | not offered | insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |
| enhanced_due_diligence | 6 | **offered** | medium, high, critical | analyst | enhanced_due_diligence |
| escalate | 7 | **offered** | high, critical | compliance | analyst_review_required |
| reject | 8 | **offered** | medium, high, critical | analyst | rejected |

|---|---|

Everything an applicant receives comes from `kb/message_template.csv`. The wording is the template's; only its declared placeholders are filled, from facts a customer may be told. Every rendered message is scanned for restricted wording before it can be sent.

## WAL-ONB-0005 - Quillon Marine Services Ltd

*Offshore vessel chartering and crew logistics* &middot; private_limited_company in GB, registered as `UK-99017733` &middot; expected usage: Up to 40 cards; around 120000 GBP per month on fuel, port fees and crew travel

### A. Classification

Intake reads `kb/applicant_type_rules.csv` in priority order and takes the **first** rule whose conditions all hold. Later rules are never looked at.

The values this application was judged on:

| Value | This application |
|---|---|
| `entity_type` | private_limited_company |
| `programme_type` | direct |
| `ownership_layers` | 1 |
| `has_corporate_shareholder` | False |
| `has_trust_or_nominee` | False |

| Rule | Order | Condition in plain words | Would give | What happened |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AT-01 | 1 | programme type is exactly `white_label` | white_label_partner | did not match: programme type was `direct` |
| AT-02 | 2 | entity type is one of `sole_trader`, `freelancer`, `self_employed` | freelancer_sole_trader | did not match: entity type was `private_limited_company` |
| AT-03 | 3 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** ownership layers is greater than 1 | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: ownership layers was `1` |

### D. People and ownership

A *beneficial owner* is the person who ultimately owns or controls the applicant. Where the holding runs through another company, the percentage is multiplied along the chain rather than read off any one document.

| Id | Name | Role | Date of birth | Nationality | Resident in |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IND-0010 | Gareth Pennyfold | director | 1975-09-17 | GB | GB |
| IND-0011 | Ines Carrasco-Bell | director | 1983-04-03 | ES | GB |
| IND-0012 | Aurelio Vantano | ubo | 1962-11-28 | IT | MT |

The threshold is **25%**: at or above it, an owner must be verified.

| Id | Owner | Control | Path | The multiplication | Declared | Verified? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UBO-0004 | Aurelio Vantano | direct shareholding | Quillon Marine Services Ltd | 55% held directly | 55% | verified |

| RS-19 | document_quality | document manual review | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |

**The arithmetic.**

```
8 + 8 + 30 + 20 = 66
```

| What | Value |
|---|---|
| Score | 66 |
| Band the score alone would give | `high` (50-79) |
| Hard floors that applied | `pep_match` forces at least **high** |
| **Final band** | **high** |
| Recommended action | enhanced_due_diligence |
| Human sign-off required | yes |

A **hard floor** is a rule that overrides the arithmetic. Where a floor applies, the band is guaranteed whatever the points say - which is why the score and the band can disagree.

### G. Decision

`kb/analyst_decision_taxonomy.csv` says which decisions exist at which band and who may record them. A decision outside the band is never offered; a decision the person's role does not allow is refused when they try to record it.

| Decision | Severity rank | At this band | Allowed at | Role needed | Resulting status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| approve | 1 | not offered | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| conditional_approve | 2 | not offered | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| withdrawn | 3 | **offered** | low, medium, high, critical, insufficient_evidence | analyst | closed_withdrawn |
| request_more_information | 4 | **offered** | low, medium, high, insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |
| insufficient_evidence | 5 | not offered | insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |
| enhanced_due_diligence | 6 | **offered** | medium, high, critical | analyst | enhanced_due_diligence |

|---|---|

## WAL-ONB-0006 - Saarvik Metall OU

*Metal fabrication and industrial subcontracting* &middot; private_limited_company in EE, registered as `EE-90014782` &middot; expected usage: Up to 20 cards; around 45000 EUR per month on materials and fuel

### A. Classification

Intake reads `kb/applicant_type_rules.csv` in priority order and takes the **first** rule whose conditions all hold. Later rules are never looked at.

The values this application was judged on:

| Value | This application |
|---|---|
| `entity_type` | private_limited_company |
| `programme_type` | direct |
| `ownership_layers` | 1 |
| `has_corporate_shareholder` | False |
| `has_trust_or_nominee` | False |

| Rule | Order | Condition in plain words | Would give | What happened |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AT-01 | 1 | programme type is exactly `white_label` | white_label_partner | did not match: programme type was `direct` |
| AT-02 | 2 | entity type is one of `sole_trader`, `freelancer`, `self_employed` | freelancer_sole_trader | did not match: entity type was `private_limited_company` |
| AT-03 | 3 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** ownership layers is greater than 1 | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: ownership layers was `1` |
| AT-04 | 4 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has corporate shareholder is true | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: has corporate shareholder was `False` |
| AT-05 | 5 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has trust or nominee is true | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: has trust or nominee was `False` |
| AT-06 | 6 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` | sme_corporate | **MATCHED - this rule decided the case** |

**Result: `sme_corporate`, route `primary`, by rule AT-06.** Standard company with simple direct ownership

**Jurisdiction and entity scope.** `kb/jurisdiction_routing.csv` maps the applicant's country to a rule set and to the Wallester entity that would hold the relationship.

| Country | Rule set | Entity scope | Supported | Rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| EE | EE | wallester_as | true | &mdash; |

### D. People and ownership

A *beneficial owner* is the person who ultimately owns or controls the applicant. Where the holding runs through another company, the percentage is multiplied along the chain rather than read off any one document.

| Id | Name | Role | Date of birth | Nationality | Resident in |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IND-0013 | Rasmus Kalda | director | 1977-05-21 | EE | EE |
| IND-0014 | Triin Lepik | director | 1986-10-08 | EE | EE |
| IND-0015 | Oskar Magiste | ubo | 1970-03-16 | EE | EE |

The threshold is **25%**: at or above it, an owner must be verified.

| Id | Owner | Control | Path | The multiplication | Declared | Verified? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UBO-0005 | Oskar Magiste | direct shareholding | Saarvik Metall OU | 80% held directly | 80% | verified |

**The arithmetic.**

```
60 = 60
```

| What | Value |
|---|---|
| Score | 60 |
| Band the score alone would give | `high` (50-79) |
| Hard floors that applied | `sanctions_possible_match` forces at least **critical** |
| **Final band** | **critical** |
| Recommended action | escalate |
| Human sign-off required | yes |

A **hard floor** is a rule that overrides the arithmetic. Where a floor applies, the band is guaranteed whatever the points say - which is why the score and the band can disagree.

### G. Decision

`kb/analyst_decision_taxonomy.csv` says which decisions exist at which band and who may record them. A decision outside the band is never offered; a decision the person's role does not allow is refused when they try to record it.

At band **`critical`**:

| Decision | Severity rank | At this band | Allowed at | Role needed | Resulting status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| approve | 1 | not offered | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| conditional_approve | 2 | not offered | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| withdrawn | 3 | **offered** | low, medium, high, critical, insufficient_evidence | analyst | closed_withdrawn |
| request_more_information | 4 | not offered | low, medium, high, insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |

### H. Communications

Everything an applicant receives comes from `kb/message_template.csv`. The wording is the template's; only its declared placeholders are filled, from facts a customer may be told. Every rendered message is scanned for restricted wording before it can be sent.

A **hold** is a lock. Only the step that placed it, or the named person it belongs to, can release it, and the case moves only when every hold is clear.

This case is marked **restricted**: customer messages are limited to generic templates.

## WAL-ONB-0007 - Bramforth Aggregates Ltd

*Supply of construction aggregates and tipper haulage* &middot; private_limited_company in GB, registered as `UK-99021560` &middot; expected usage: Up to 25 cards; around 55000 GBP per month on fuel and plant hire

### A. Classification

Intake reads `kb/applicant_type_rules.csv` in priority order and takes the **first** rule whose conditions all hold. Later rules are never looked at.

The values this application was judged on:

| Value | This application |
|---|---|
| `entity_type` | private_limited_company |
| `programme_type` | direct |
| `ownership_layers` | 1 |
| `has_corporate_shareholder` | False |
| `has_trust_or_nominee` | False |

| Rule | Order | Condition in plain words | Would give | What happened |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AT-01 | 1 | programme type is exactly `white_label` | white_label_partner | did not match: programme type was `direct` |
| AT-02 | 2 | entity type is one of `sole_trader`, `freelancer`, `self_employed` | freelancer_sole_trader | did not match: entity type was `private_limited_company` |
| AT-03 | 3 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** ownership layers is greater than 1 | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: ownership layers was `1` |
| AT-04 | 4 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has corporate shareholder is true | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: has corporate shareholder was `False` |
| AT-05 | 5 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has trust or nominee is true | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: has trust or nominee was `False` |
| AT-06 | 6 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` | sme_corporate | **MATCHED - this rule decided the case** |

**Result: `sme_corporate`, route `primary`, by rule AT-06.** Standard company with simple direct ownership

**Jurisdiction and entity scope.** `kb/jurisdiction_routing.csv` maps the applicant's country to a rule set and to the Wallester entity that would hold the relationship.

| Country | Rule set | Entity scope | Supported | Rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GB | UK | wallester_uk_ltd | true | &mdash; |

### D. People and ownership

A *beneficial owner* is the person who ultimately owns or controls the applicant. Where the holding runs through another company, the percentage is multiplied along the chain rather than read off any one document.

| Id | Name | Role | Date of birth | Nationality | Resident in |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IND-0016 | Nigel Braithwaite-Cole | director | 1968-04-12 | GB | GB |
| IND-0017 | Fenella Quist | ubo | 1979-01-25 | GB | GB |

The threshold is **25%**: at or above it, an owner must be verified.

| Id | Owner | Control | Path | The multiplication | Declared | Verified? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UBO-0006 | Fenella Quist | direct shareholding | Bramforth Aggregates Ltd | 45% held directly | 45% | verified |

### F. Risk

Step 7 walks every factor in `kb/risk_scoring_matrix.csv` and adds the points for the ones whose condition is true on this case. The table below shows **all** of them, fired or not, so you can see what was considered and not only what counted.

| RS-05 | registry_address_mismatch | registry address mismatch | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |

| RS-08 | identity_verification | identity check failed | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-09 | identity_verification | identity duplicate | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-10 | extraction_confidence | low confidence accepted as read | **fired** | +6 | 6 | FLD-0138 |
| RS-11 | extraction_confidence | field corrected by analyst | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-12 | usage_volume | expected monthly spend above 50k | **fired** | +8 | 14 | APP-0007 |
| RS-13 | pep_exposure | pep match | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-14 | adverse_media_severity | adverse media moderate | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-15 | adverse_media_severity | adverse media serious | **fired** | +40 | 54 | SCR-0020 |
| RS-16 | sanctions_exposure | sanctions possible match | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-17 | sanctions_exposure | sanctions clear match | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-18 | document_quality | document resubmission required | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-19 | document_quality | document manual review | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |

**The arithmetic.**

```
6 + 8 + 40 = 54
```

| What | Value |
|---|---|
| Score | 54 |
| Band the score alone would give | `high` (50-79) |
| Hard floors that applied | `adverse_media_serious` forces at least **high** |
| **Final band** | **high** |
| Recommended action | enhanced_due_diligence |
| Human sign-off required | yes |

A **hard floor** is a rule that overrides the arithmetic. Where a floor applies, the band is guaranteed whatever the points say - which is why the score and the band can disagree.

### G. Decision

`kb/analyst_decision_taxonomy.csv` says which decisions exist at which band and who may record them. A decision outside the band is never offered; a decision the person's role does not allow is refused when they try to record it.

At band **`high`**:

| Decision | Severity rank | At this band | Allowed at | Role needed | Resulting status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| approve | 1 | not offered | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| conditional_approve | 2 | not offered | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| withdrawn | 3 | **offered** | low, medium, high, critical, insufficient_evidence | analyst | closed_withdrawn |
| request_more_information | 4 | **offered** | low, medium, high, insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |
| insufficient_evidence | 5 | not offered | insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |
| enhanced_due_diligence | 6 | **offered** | medium, high, critical | analyst | enhanced_due_diligence |
| escalate | 7 | **offered** | high, critical | compliance | analyst_review_required |
| reject | 8 | **offered** | medium, high, critical | analyst | rejected |

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Decision | **escalate** |
| Recorded by | D. Ferreira (compliance) |
| Reason code | serious_adverse_media |
| Rationale | Serious adverse media on the sole director and sole signatory, in the applicant's own sector, with no separation of control. Referring to the financial crime review team rather than handling in standard enhanced due diligence. |
| Evidence relied on | RSK-0006, REG-0006, EVP-0006 |
| Escalation target | Financial crime review team - Wallester UK Ltd |
| Override? | yes |
| Override direction | stricter |
| Override reason | Serious adverse media on the sole director and sole signatory, in the applicant's own sector, with no separation of control. Referring to the financial crime review team rather than handling in standard enhanced due diligence. |

An **override** means the decision differed from the recommendation. The system recommended `enhanced_due_diligence` (severity rank 6) and the reviewer chose `escalate` (rank 7), so the direction is **stricter**. The reviewer does not declare this; the system computes it from the severity order.

### H. Communications

Everything an applicant receives comes from `kb/message_template.csv`. The wording is the template's; only its declared placeholders are filled, from facts a customer may be told. Every rendered message is scanned for restricted wording before it can be sent.

> This case carries a **restricted finding**, so only the generic templates - the ones that state no reason - were available to it.

| Template | TPL-0004 |

## WAL-ONB-0010 - Thornbury Analytics Ltd

*Data analytics consultancy for the retail sector* &middot; private_limited_company in GB, registered as `UK-99025819` &middot; expected usage: Up to 18 cards; around 22000 GBP per month on software and travel

### A. Classification

Intake reads `kb/applicant_type_rules.csv` in priority order and takes the **first** rule whose conditions all hold. Later rules are never looked at.

The values this application was judged on:

| Value | This application |
|---|---|
| `entity_type` | private_limited_company |
| `programme_type` | direct |
| `ownership_layers` | 2 |
| `has_corporate_shareholder` | True |
| `has_trust_or_nominee` | False |

| Rule | Order | Condition in plain words | Would give | What happened |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AT-01 | 1 | programme type is exactly `white_label` | white_label_partner | did not match: programme type was `direct` |
| AT-02 | 2 | entity type is one of `sole_trader`, `freelancer`, `self_employed` | freelancer_sole_trader | did not match: entity type was `private_limited_company` |
| AT-03 | 3 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** ownership layers is greater than 1 | complex_corporate_ubo | **MATCHED - this rule decided the case** |
| AT-04 | 4 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has corporate shareholder is true | complex_corporate_ubo | not checked - an earlier rule already matched |
| AT-05 | 5 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has trust or nominee is true | complex_corporate_ubo | not checked - an earlier rule already matched |
| AT-06 | 6 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` | sme_corporate | not checked - an earlier rule already matched |

**Result: `complex_corporate_ubo`, route `primary`, by rule AT-03.** Indirect ownership chain needs enhanced UBO handling (Sections 5.1 and 14.4)

### D. People and ownership

A *beneficial owner* is the person who ultimately owns or controls the applicant. Where the holding runs through another company, the percentage is multiplied along the chain rather than read off any one document.

| Id | Name | Role | Date of birth | Nationality | Resident in |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IND-0021 | Imogen Skellow | director | 1987-12-01 | GB | GB |
| IND-0022 | Callum Pryde-Nash | director | 1979-07-23 | GB | GB |
| IND-0023 | Helena Marchbank | ubo | 1973-10-14 | GB | GB |

The threshold is **25%**: at or above it, an owner must be verified.

| Id | Owner | Control | Path | The multiplication | Declared | Verified? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UBO-0008 | Helena Marchbank | indirect shareholding | Marchbank Holdings Ltd > Thornbury Analytics Ltd | 100% x 70% = **70%** | 70% | verified |

### G. Decision

`kb/analyst_decision_taxonomy.csv` says which decisions exist at which band and who may record them. A decision outside the band is never offered; a decision the person's role does not allow is refused when they try to record it.

At band **`low`**:

| Decision | Severity rank | At this band | Allowed at | Role needed | Resulting status |

| approve | 1 | **offered** | low, medium | analyst | approved |

| withdrawn | 3 | **offered** | low, medium, high, critical, insufficient_evidence | analyst | closed_withdrawn |

| insufficient_evidence | 5 | not offered | insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |

## WAL-ONB-0011 - Ardenhall Joinery Ltd

*Bespoke joinery and shopfitting for retail interiors* &middot; private_limited_company in GB, registered as `UK-99031147` &middot; expected usage: Up to 10 cards; around 18000 GBP per month on timber and fixings

### A. Classification

Intake reads `kb/applicant_type_rules.csv` in priority order and takes the **first** rule whose conditions all hold. Later rules are never looked at.

The values this application was judged on:

| Value | This application |
|---|---|
| `entity_type` | private_limited_company |
| `programme_type` | direct |
| `ownership_layers` | 1 |
| `has_corporate_shareholder` | False |
| `has_trust_or_nominee` | False |

| Rule | Order | Condition in plain words | Would give | What happened |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AT-01 | 1 | programme type is exactly `white_label` | white_label_partner | did not match: programme type was `direct` |
| AT-02 | 2 | entity type is one of `sole_trader`, `freelancer`, `self_employed` | freelancer_sole_trader | did not match: entity type was `private_limited_company` |
| AT-03 | 3 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** ownership layers is greater than 1 | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: ownership layers was `1` |
| AT-04 | 4 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has corporate shareholder is true | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: has corporate shareholder was `False` |
| AT-05 | 5 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has trust or nominee is true | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: has trust or nominee was `False` |
| AT-06 | 6 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` | sme_corporate | **MATCHED - this rule decided the case** |

**Result: `sme_corporate`, route `primary`, by rule AT-06.** Standard company with simple direct ownership

**Jurisdiction and entity scope.** `kb/jurisdiction_routing.csv` maps the applicant's country to a rule set and to the Wallester entity that would hold the relationship.

| Country | Rule set | Entity scope | Supported | Rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GB | UK | wallester_uk_ltd | true | &mdash; |

### D. People and ownership

A *beneficial owner* is the person who ultimately owns or controls the applicant. Where the holding runs through another company, the percentage is multiplied along the chain rather than read off any one document.

| Id | Name | Role | Date of birth | Nationality | Resident in |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IND-0024 | Rowan Feathersby | director | 1972-03-08 | GB | GB |
| IND-0025 | Delia Ormsby-Crane | director | 1980-11-26 | GB | GB |
| IND-0026 | Wilhelmina Tarrant | ubo | 1965-07-14 | GB | GB |

The threshold is **25%**: at or above it, an owner must be verified.

| Id | Owner | Control | Path | The multiplication | Declared | Verified? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UBO-0009 | Wilhelmina Tarrant | direct shareholding | Ardenhall Joinery Ltd | 65% held directly | 65% | verified |

**The arithmetic.**

```
60 = 60
```

| What | Value |
|---|---|
| Score | 60 |
| Band the score alone would give | `high` (50-79) |
| Hard floors that applied | `entity_not_active` forces at least **high** |
| **Final band** | **high** |
| Recommended action | reject |
| Human sign-off required | yes |

A **hard floor** is a rule that overrides the arithmetic. Where a floor applies, the band is guaranteed whatever the points say - which is why the score and the band can disagree.

### G. Decision

`kb/analyst_decision_taxonomy.csv` says which decisions exist at which band and who may record them. A decision outside the band is never offered; a decision the person's role does not allow is refused when they try to record it.

| Decision | Severity rank | At this band | Allowed at | Role needed | Resulting status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| approve | 1 | not offered | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| conditional_approve | 2 | not offered | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| withdrawn | 3 | **offered** | low, medium, high, critical, insufficient_evidence | analyst | closed_withdrawn |
| request_more_information | 4 | **offered** | low, medium, high, insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |
| insufficient_evidence | 5 | not offered | insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |
| enhanced_due_diligence | 6 | **offered** | medium, high, critical | analyst | enhanced_due_diligence |
| escalate | 7 | **offered** | high, critical | compliance | analyst_review_required |
| reject | 8 | **offered** | medium, high, critical | analyst | rejected |

| Field | Value |
|---|---|

### J. Dashboard line

Exactly what the operations dashboard shows for this case. A dash in Band or Score means the case never reached risk scoring - it is not a score of zero.

| WAL-ONB-0011 | Ardenhall Joinery Ltd | sme_corporate | rejected | system | high | 60 | 1 | eligibility (analyst) | 0 |

The open holds in full:

| Hold | Code | Owner | Placed by | Why |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| HLD-0008 | eligibility | analyst | step.verification | 1 verification outcome(s) need an analyst: RG-01: Company status on the register is 'dissolved': A dissolved company cannot be onboarded; an analyst confirms before anything is said to the customer |

A **hold** is a lock. Only the step that placed it, or the named person it belongs to, can release it, and the case moves only when every hold is clear.

## WAL-ONB-0012 - Solvarn Baltic OU

*Industrial valve distribution and maintenance* &middot; private_limited_company in EE, registered as `EE-90021455` &middot; expected usage: Up to 16 cards; around 30000 EUR per month on parts and travel

### A. Classification

Intake reads `kb/applicant_type_rules.csv` in priority order and takes the **first** rule whose conditions all hold. Later rules are never looked at.

The values this application was judged on:

| Value | This application |
|---|---|
| `entity_type` | private_limited_company |
| `programme_type` | direct |
| `ownership_layers` | 1 |
| `has_corporate_shareholder` | False |
| `has_trust_or_nominee` | False |

| Rule | Order | Condition in plain words | Would give | What happened |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AT-01 | 1 | programme type is exactly `white_label` | white_label_partner | did not match: programme type was `direct` |
| AT-02 | 2 | entity type is one of `sole_trader`, `freelancer`, `self_employed` | freelancer_sole_trader | did not match: entity type was `private_limited_company` |
| AT-03 | 3 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** ownership layers is greater than 1 | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: ownership layers was `1` |
| AT-04 | 4 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has corporate shareholder is true | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: has corporate shareholder was `False` |
| AT-05 | 5 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has trust or nominee is true | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: has trust or nominee was `False` |
| AT-06 | 6 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` | sme_corporate | **MATCHED - this rule decided the case** |

**Result: `sme_corporate`, route `primary`, by rule AT-06.** Standard company with simple direct ownership

**Jurisdiction and entity scope.** `kb/jurisdiction_routing.csv` maps the applicant's country to a rule set and to the Wallester entity that would hold the relationship.

| Country | Rule set | Entity scope | Supported | Rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| EE | EE | wallester_as | true | &mdash; |

### D. People and ownership

A *beneficial owner* is the person who ultimately owns or controls the applicant. Where the holding runs through another company, the percentage is multiplied along the chain rather than read off any one document.

| Id | Name | Role | Date of birth | Nationality | Resident in |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IND-0027 | Taavi Ruumet | director | 1979-05-30 | EE | EE |
| IND-0028 | Ivar Kolmsaar | ubo | 1968-02-17 | EE | EE |

The threshold is **25%**: at or above it, an owner must be verified.

| Id | Owner | Control | Path | The multiplication | Declared | Verified? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UBO-0010 | Ivar Kolmsaar | direct shareholding | Solvarn Baltic OU | 80% held directly | 80% | verified |

### F. Risk

Step 7 walks every factor in `kb/risk_scoring_matrix.csv` and adds the points for the ones whose condition is true on this case. The table below shows **all** of them, fired or not, so you can see what was considered and not only what counted.

| Id | Factor | Condition | On this case | Points added | Running total | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RS-01 | jurisdiction_risk | ubo resident outside applicant country | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-02 | industry_risk | high risk industry | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-03 | ownership_complexity | ownership layers gt 1 | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-04 | ownership_opacity | ubo not supported by registry | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-05 | registry_address_mismatch | registry address mismatch | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-06 | registry_director_mismatch | registry director mismatch | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-07 | entity_status | entity not active | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-08 | identity_verification | identity check failed | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-09 | identity_verification | identity duplicate | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-10 | extraction_confidence | low confidence accepted as read | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-11 | extraction_confidence | field corrected by analyst | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-12 | usage_volume | expected monthly spend above 50k | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-13 | pep_exposure | pep match | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-14 | adverse_media_severity | adverse media moderate | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-15 | adverse_media_severity | adverse media serious | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-16 | sanctions_exposure | sanctions possible match | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-17 | sanctions_exposure | sanctions clear match | **fired** | +70 | 70 | SCR-0035 |
| RS-18 | document_quality | document resubmission required | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |
| RS-19 | document_quality | document manual review | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |

**The arithmetic.**

```
70 = 70
```

| What | Value |
|---|---|
| Score | 70 |
| Band the score alone would give | `high` (50-79) |
| Hard floors that applied | `sanctions_clear_match` forces at least **critical** |
| **Final band** | **critical** |
| Recommended action | escalate |
| Human sign-off required | yes |

A **hard floor** is a rule that overrides the arithmetic. Where a floor applies, the band is guaranteed whatever the points say - which is why the score and the band can disagree.

### G. Decision

`kb/analyst_decision_taxonomy.csv` says which decisions exist at which band and who may record them. A decision outside the band is never offered; a decision the person's role does not allow is refused when they try to record it.

At band **`critical`**:

| Decision | Severity rank | At this band | Allowed at | Role needed | Resulting status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| approve | 1 | not offered | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| conditional_approve | 2 | not offered | low, medium | analyst | approved |
| withdrawn | 3 | **offered** | low, medium, high, critical, insufficient_evidence | analyst | closed_withdrawn |
| request_more_information | 4 | not offered | low, medium, high, insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |
| insufficient_evidence | 5 | not offered | insufficient_evidence | analyst | analyst_review_required |
| enhanced_due_diligence | 6 | **offered** | medium, high, critical | analyst | enhanced_due_diligence |
| escalate | 7 | **offered** | high, critical | compliance | analyst_review_required |
| reject | 8 | **offered** | medium, high, critical | analyst | rejected |

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Decision | **escalate** |

| Reason code | confirmed_sanctions_match_on_ubo |

## WAL-ONB-0013 - Pemberly Tooling Ltd

*Precision tooling and machine parts for light manufacturing* &middot; private_limited_company in GB, registered as `UK-99034820` &middot; expected usage: Up to 14 cards; around 21000 GBP per month on materials and freight

### A. Classification

Intake reads `kb/applicant_type_rules.csv` in priority order and takes the **first** rule whose conditions all hold. Later rules are never looked at.

The values this application was judged on:

| Value | This application |
|---|---|
| `entity_type` | private_limited_company |
| `programme_type` | direct |
| `ownership_layers` | 1 |
| `has_corporate_shareholder` | False |
| `has_trust_or_nominee` | False |

| Rule | Order | Condition in plain words | Would give | What happened |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AT-01 | 1 | programme type is exactly `white_label` | white_label_partner | did not match: programme type was `direct` |
| AT-02 | 2 | entity type is one of `sole_trader`, `freelancer`, `self_employed` | freelancer_sole_trader | did not match: entity type was `private_limited_company` |
| AT-03 | 3 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** ownership layers is greater than 1 | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: ownership layers was `1` |
| AT-04 | 4 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has corporate shareholder is true | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: has corporate shareholder was `False` |
| AT-05 | 5 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has trust or nominee is true | complex_corporate_ubo | did not match: has trust or nominee was `False` |
| AT-06 | 6 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` | sme_corporate | **MATCHED - this rule decided the case** |

**Result: `sme_corporate`, route `primary`, by rule AT-06.** Standard company with simple direct ownership

### D. People and ownership

A *beneficial owner* is the person who ultimately owns or controls the applicant. Where the holding runs through another company, the percentage is multiplied along the chain rather than read off any one document.

| Id | Name | Role | Date of birth | Nationality | Resident in |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IND-0029 | Hadley Winstock | director | 1976-10-03 | GB | GB |
| IND-0030 | Marguerite Ellcott | director | 1983-06-21 | GB | GB |
| IND-0031 | Oswin Prattley | ubo | 1970-12-09 | GB | GB |

The threshold is **25%**: at or above it, an owner must be verified.

| Id | Owner | Control | Path | The multiplication | Declared | Verified? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UBO-0011 | Oswin Prattley | direct shareholding | Pemberly Tooling Ltd | 55% held directly | 55% | verified |

| RS-19 | document_quality | document manual review | not fired | 0 |  | &mdash; |

**The arithmetic.**

```
25 = 25
```

The factors above come to 25, but **this case carries no score at all.** An unresolved gap in the evidence means there is not enough to score: a number here would read as a risk level when the honest answer is that nobody knows yet. The band carries that instead.

| What | Value |
|---|---|
| Score | &mdash; |
| Band the score alone would give | none - not scored |
| Hard floors that applied | `insufficient_evidence_hold` forces at least **insufficient_evidence** |
| **Final band** | **insufficient_evidence** |
| Recommended action | insufficient_evidence |
| Human sign-off required | yes |

A **hard floor** is a rule that overrides the arithmetic. Where a floor applies, the band is guaranteed whatever the points say - which is why the score and the band can disagree.

### G. Decision

## WAL-ONB-0014 - Elise Kaarma

*Studio ceramics and small-batch tableware* &middot; sole_trader in EE, registered as `EE-FIE-4471208` &middot; expected usage: One card; up to 1500 EUR per month on clay, glazes and kiln hire

### A. Classification

Intake reads `kb/applicant_type_rules.csv` in priority order and takes the **first** rule whose conditions all hold. Later rules are never looked at.

The values this application was judged on:

|---|---|
| `entity_type` | sole_trader |
| `programme_type` | direct |
| `ownership_layers` | 0 |
| `has_corporate_shareholder` | False |
| `has_trust_or_nominee` | False |

| Rule | Order | Condition in plain words | Would give | What happened |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AT-01 | 1 | programme type is exactly `white_label` | white_label_partner | did not match: programme type was `direct` |
| AT-02 | 2 | entity type is one of `sole_trader`, `freelancer`, `self_employed` | freelancer_sole_trader | **MATCHED - this rule decided the case** |
| AT-03 | 3 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** ownership layers is greater than 1 | complex_corporate_ubo | not checked - an earlier rule already matched |
| AT-04 | 4 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has corporate shareholder is true | complex_corporate_ubo | not checked - an earlier rule already matched |

| AT-06 | 6 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` | sme_corporate | not checked - an earlier rule already matched |

## Appendix - the knowledge base

Every rule the pipeline follows lives in a CSV under `kb/`. Changing a rule is an edit to one of these files and a version bump in `kb/kb_manifest.json` - no code change and no release. This appendix prints them as they stand at **kb-2026.09-poc-v8**.

### Applicant-type rules

Checked in priority order. The **first** rule whose conditions all hold decides the applicant type, and the rest are never evaluated - which is why the order matters as much as the conditions.

| Order | Rule | Condition in plain words | Applicant type | Route | Why it exists |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | AT-01 | programme type is exactly `white_label` | white_label_partner | white_label_branch | White-label programme partners are identified but not decisioned in the first POC (Sections 3 and 6.2) |
| 2 | AT-02 | entity type is one of `sole_trader`, `freelancer`, `self_employed` | freelancer_sole_trader | primary | Individuals trading on their own account (Section 5.1) |
| 3 | AT-03 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** ownership layers is greater than 1 | complex_corporate_ubo | primary | Indirect ownership chain needs enhanced UBO handling (Sections 5.1 and 14.4) |
| 4 | AT-04 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has corporate shareholder is true | complex_corporate_ubo | primary | A company owns part of the applicant |
| 5 | AT-05 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` **and** has trust or nominee is true | complex_corporate_ubo | primary | Trust or nominee arrangements need explanation (Section 5.2) |
| 6 | AT-06 | entity type is one of `private_limited`, `private_limited_company`, `public_limited`, `partnership`, `llp` | sme_corporate | primary | Standard company with simple direct ownership |

> The last rule has no extra condition, so every company reaches it eventually. It only ever wins because the rules above it were tried first.

### Jurisdiction routing

The applicant's country decides which rule set applies and which Wallester entity would hold the relationship.

| Country | Rule | Rule set | Entity scope | Supported | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DE |  | EE | wallester_as | true |  |
| EE |  | EE | wallester_as | true |  |

### Risk scoring matrix

Each factor adds its points when its condition is true on the case. **Every weight is a POC placeholder** - the brief does not state them, so they were set so the scripted dataset reproduces, and each carries that status in the file itself.

| Id | Factor | Reads from | Condition in plain words | Points | What it means | Weight status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RS-01 | jurisdiction_risk | individual | ubo resident outside applicant country | 8 | A beneficial owner lives outside the country the applicant is registered in | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-02 | industry_risk | registry check | high risk industry | 12 | The register or the declared activity marks this as a higher-risk industry | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-03 | ownership_complexity | ubo | ownership layers gt 1 | 15 | Ownership runs through at least one intermediate company | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-04 | ownership_opacity | finding | ubo not supported by registry | 25 | The register does not corroborate the declared beneficial ownership | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-05 | registry_address_mismatch | finding | registry address mismatch | 30 | The registered address on file does not match the register | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-06 | registry_director_mismatch | finding | registry director mismatch | 12 | The declared directors do not match the register | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-07 | entity_status | finding | entity not active | 60 | The register does not show the entity as active | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-08 | identity_verification | identity check | identity check failed | 25 | An identity check returned fail or review for a director, owner or signatory | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-09 | identity_verification | identity check | identity duplicate | 25 | An individual appears to duplicate someone already known to us | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-10 | extraction_confidence | extracted field | low confidence accepted as read | 6 | A value was read below the confidence floor and accepted as read by an analyst | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-11 | extraction_confidence | extracted field | field corrected by analyst | 5 | An analyst had to correct a value the system could not read reliably | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-12 | usage_volume | applicant | expected monthly spend above 50k | 8 | Expected monthly card spend is above the 50000 threshold | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-13 | pep_exposure | screening check | pep match | 30 | A subject is a politically exposed person or a close associate | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-14 | adverse_media_severity | screening check | adverse media moderate | 20 | Moderate adverse media of relevance to a subject; allegations, not established fact | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-15 | adverse_media_severity | screening check | adverse media serious | 40 | Serious adverse media of relevance to a subject; allegations, not established fact | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-16 | sanctions_exposure | screening check | sanctions possible match | 60 | A possible sanctions match that only a human can resolve | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-17 | sanctions_exposure | screening check | sanctions clear match | 70 | A confirmed sanctions match | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-18 | document_quality | document | document resubmission required | 10 | A document failed the quality screen and had to be sent back | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |
| RS-19 | document_quality | document | document manual review | 15 | A document was held for an analyst at the quality screen | poc_placeholder - Wallester to confirm |

### Risk bands and hard floors

A band is either a score range, or a **hard floor** - a condition that forces a minimum band whatever the score says. Where a floor applies, the score explains the case and the floor decides it.

**Bands by score**

| Id | Band | From | To | Recommended action | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RB-01 | low | 0 | 24 | approve | Nothing outstanding. Still requires a human decision; there is no automatic approval |
| RB-02 | medium | 25 | 49 | conditional_approve | Something to resolve, but nothing that stops the application |
| RB-03 | high | 50 | 79 | enhanced_due_diligence | Enhanced due diligence before any decision |
| RB-04 | critical | 80 | 100 | escalate | Compliance decision required |

**Hard floors**

| Id | What triggers it | Forces at least | Recommended action | Why |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| RB-10 | entity not active | high | reject | A blocking registry failure is an eligibility failure: no amount of further diligence changes the answer, so the recommendation is to reject whatever the score |
| RB-05 | sanctions clear match | critical | escalate | A confirmed sanctions match is critical whatever the score |
| RB-06 | sanctions possible match | critical | escalate | An unresolved possible sanctions match is critical whatever the score |
| RB-07 | pep match | high | enhanced_due_diligence | A PEP match is at least high whatever the score |
| RB-08 | adverse media serious | high | enhanced_due_diligence | Serious adverse media is at least high whatever the score |
| RB-09 | insufficient evidence hold | insufficient_evidence | insufficient_evidence | An unresolved gap in the evidence. The case is not scored: there is not enough to score |

### Decision taxonomy

Which decisions exist, where they are offered, who may record them, and how strict each one is. The **severity rank** is what lets the system work out whether a reviewer was stricter or more lenient than the recommendation, without anyone having to say so.

| Decision | Severity rank | Allowed at bands | Role needed | Reason required | Resulting status | Customer template |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| approve | 1 | low, medium | analyst | true | approved | TPL-0007 |
| conditional_approve | 2 | low, medium | analyst | true | approved | TPL-0007 |
| withdrawn | 3 | low, medium, high, critical, insufficient_evidence | analyst | true | closed_withdrawn | TPL-0012 |
| request_more_information | 4 | low, medium, high, insufficient_evidence | analyst | true | analyst_review_required | TPL-0002 |
| insufficient_evidence | 5 | insufficient_evidence | analyst | true | analyst_review_required | TPL-0002 |
| enhanced_due_diligence | 6 | medium, high, critical | analyst | true | enhanced_due_diligence | TPL-0004 |
| escalate | 7 | high, critical | compliance | true | analyst_review_required | TPL-0004 |
| reject | 8 | medium, high, critical | analyst | true | rejected | TPL-0011 |
